### Windows rc.2 row and triage

Windows arm `41115849156` (dispatched 19:30:44Z, issue_comment `test-windows` on #657) posted the signed PASS-with-findings report as owner-authored comment `6532711345` at 20:23:03Z, then failed its own forward step:

- 20:22:39Z the agent wrote the run-bound decision (`action: maintainer`).
- 20:23:02Z `Verify test decided` accepted it (`Windows tester decision and findings report present`).
- 20:23:05Z `Forward Windows tester decision` rejected it: `Tester decision file lost this run's contract nonce before the handoff`.
- No writer ran between the verify and the forward; only the findings poster ran. The likely cause is a CR-bearing `jq -r` output under the pinned trusted PATH (the verify uses the default PATH; the forward uses `steps.trusted_path.outputs.path`, which begins with `/ucrt64/bin`). Filed as a new infra issue for the Lab Engineer; no product binary impact.

Report findings (full text in comment `6532711345`):

- F1 high, docs: the site, README, and docs still point at rc.1 and old run `40161835683`; the rc.2 desktop zip itself ships the stale `docs/desktop.md` as `README-desktop.md`, which only a rebuilt candidate can fix. The tree copy is covered by #789.
- F2 medium, site: the `releases.html` current nav pill does not scroll into view at 390x844 (`scrollLeft 0.0`), reproduced on Windows Chrome. Covered by #788.
- W1 medium, harness: `run-windows.ps1` recorded `disposition: pass` from a 110x30 host showing only the minimum-size card; 2 of 7 phases captured, later phases `bytes=0`, `tier_observed: null`, `build.recorded: false`. Filed as a new Maze Chase issue.
- W2 medium, harness: compat unit suite fails 4/53 on Windows (`termios` import, NTFS executable bit, macOS separator not skipped). Same new issue.
- W3 medium, harness: documented `python3 -m http.server` site E2E path aborts in undici `Parser.finish` on Node 22.23.4; a Node HTTP/1.1 server completes the crawl. Same new issue, coordinating with #786/#788.
- Low observations: an invalid or file-valued terminal `--dir` silently does not persist settings; Windows docs name SmartScreen only for the binary, not the unsigned setup. Not blockers.

Per-target groups kept their actions non-conflicting; this run dispatched no lane.

### Follow-up filed (the one substantive act)

`create_issue`: `[Infra] setup.sh first-run path breaks on a standard clone URL: repo name keeps .git, so --secrets and --dispatch target a 404`.

Verified on `main` `f3e28d3d`:

- `setup.sh:90` `sed -E 's#.*[:/]([^/]+/[^/]+)(\.git)?$#\1#'` is greedy, so `https://github.com/owner/repo.git` and `git@github.com:owner/repo.git` both yield `owner/repo.git` (only the suffix-free URL is correct). `--secrets` then runs `gh secret set --repo owner/repo.git`, which 404s, and `set -euo pipefail` aborts before `--dispatch`; the `gh secret list` probe at `setup.sh:100` hides it.
- A tree with no `origin` and `GITHUB_REPOSITORY` unset aborts at the assignment with exit 2 (pipefail propagates git's failure) instead of warning and continuing. Verified in `/tmp/setupcheck` (`env -u GITHUB_REPOSITORY bash setup.sh` -> rc 2, no message).
- Scoped the eval's pre-existing doc drift in the same issue: `docs/index.html:214` comment label, `AGENTS.md:261`, `LAB.md:1605-1607`, `LAB.md:1617`.

The issue body states the queue order (behind #754 and #798) and forbids an immediate `lab` dispatch while delivery and the #754 quota wall are live.

### Decisions and rationale

`.maintainer/decision.json` = one `create_issue` entry. `.maintainer/comment.md` empty (target #797 is merged; the wrapper summary is the only comment). `.maintainer/personality.md` unchanged. No `emergency.json`.

The failure triage is correlated and stood down (stale head, superseded by the live fresh review; its own triage run `41183051630` is in progress).
- No `lab`: recorded order is #754, #798, then the new setup.sh issue; delivery is in flight and quota risk is live.
